AI8 · Ljudje · osnutek R2AI8 · People · draft R2

Top ljudje skozi ves časTop people of all time

Dvajset področij človeškega delovanja, na vsakem seznamu 25 oseb. Znanost je razdeljena na pet podpodročij: fiziko, astronomijo, kemijo in vede o Zemlji, biologijo in ekologijo ter matematiko. Posebno, 21. področje so Zloglasni; ta seznam je opomin, ne čast. Mesta so skupek odgovorov 15 jezikovnih modelov, ki so skupaj predlagali 1955 različnih oseb. Pri vsaki osebi so letnice, en stavek o tem, zakaj je pomembna, in ključni trenutek, od koder lahko začnemo pot.

Twenty fields of human endeavour, 25 people on each list. Science is split into five sub-fields: physics, astronomy, chemistry and Earth sciences, biology and ecology, and mathematics. A special 21st field, the Infamous, is a warning, not an honour. The places combine the answers of 15 language models, which together proposed 1,955 different people. Each person has life years, one sentence on why they matter, and a key moment where a journey can start.

Kdo je odločil kaj: pobuda, razdelitev na področja in odločitev, da osnutek gre na splet takoj in se sproti dopolnjuje, so BD-jeve. Uvrstitve so modelske. Opise, letnice in ključne trenutke je napisal Claude; BD jih še ni pregledal. Kako je seznam nastal

Who decided what: the idea, the choice of fields and the decision to put the draft online right away and keep extending it are BD's. The rankings are the models'. The descriptions, years and key moments were written by Claude; BD has not reviewed them yet. How the list was made

01 · PodročjeField

ZnanostScience

Pet podpodročij, vsako s svojim seznamom 25 oseb: fizika, astronomija, kemija in vede o Zemlji, biologija in ekologija ter matematika.

Five sub-fields, each with its own list of 25 people: physics, astronomy, chemistry and Earth sciences, biology and ecology, and mathematics.

1a · ZnanostScience

FizikaPhysics

Mehanika, elektromagnetizem, termodinamika, optika, relativnost, kvantna, jedrska in fizika delcev; astronomija ima svoje podpodročje.

Mechanics, electromagnetism, thermodynamics, optics, relativity, quantum, nuclear and particle physics; astronomy has its own sub-field.

Modeli so predlagali 59 različnih oseb; tu je prvih 25.The models proposed 59 different people; the top 25 are shown here.

  1. 1

    Albert EinsteinAlbert Einstein 1879–19551879–1955

    S teorijama relativnosti je na novo opredelil prostor, čas in gravitacijo ter pojasnil fotoelektrični pojav.

    His special and general relativity redefined space, time and gravity; he also explained the photoelectric effect.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Einstein, Bern 1905: posebna teorija relativnostiEinstein, Bern 1905: special theory of relativity

    Ø 24,0 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 24.0 points · named by 15/15

  2. 2

    Isaac NewtonIsaac Newton 1643–17271643–1727

    Postavil je zakone gibanja in zakon splošne gravitacije ter neodvisno od Leibniza razvil infinitezimalni račun.

    Formulated the laws of motion and universal gravitation and developed calculus independently of Leibniz.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Newton, London 1687: izide delo Principia o gibanju in gravitacijiNewton, London 1687: Principia on motion and gravitation published

    Ø 23,3 točke · omenilo 14/15avg 23.3 points · named by 14/15

  3. 3

    James Clerk MaxwellJames Clerk Maxwell 1831–18791831–1879

    S svojimi enačbami je poenotil elektriko, magnetizem in svetlobo ter napovedal elektromagnetno valovanje.

    Unified electricity, magnetism and light in his equations and predicted electromagnetic waves.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Maxwell, London 1864: svetloba kot elektromagnetno valovanjeMaxwell, London 1864: light as an electromagnetic wave

    Ø 22,5 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 22.5 points · named by 15/15

  4. 4

    Niels BohrNiels Bohr 1885–19621885–1962

    Razvil je kvantni model atoma in načelo komplementarnosti ter v Københavnu vodil inštitut, ki je bil središče razvoja kvantne mehanike.

    Developed the quantum model of the atom and the principle of complementarity, and led the Copenhagen institute at the centre of quantum mechanics.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Bohr, 1913: predstavi model atoma s kvantiziranimi elektronskimi orbitamiBohr, 1913: presents his atomic model with quantised electron orbits

    Ø 21,0 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 21.0 points · named by 15/15

  5. 5

    Galileo GalileiGalileo Galilei 1564–16421564–1642

    S teleskopom je odkril Jupitrove lune in mene Venere ter s poskusi postavil temelje fizike gibanja.

    Discovered Jupiter's moons and the phases of Venus by telescope and grounded the physics of motion in experiment.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Galilei, Padova 1610: odkrije štiri Jupitrove luneGalileo, Padua 1610: discovers four moons of Jupiter

    Ø 19,5 točke · omenilo 14/15avg 19.5 points · named by 14/15

  6. 6

    Werner HeisenbergWerner Heisenberg 1901–19761901–1976

    Utemeljil je matrično mehaniko in formuliral načelo nedoločenosti, enega temeljev kvantne mehanike.

    Founded matrix mechanics and formulated the uncertainty principle, one of the foundations of quantum mechanics.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Heisenberg, Helgoland 1925: zasnuje temelje matrične mehanikeHeisenberg, Heligoland 1925: conceives the foundations of matrix mechanics

    Ø 19,1 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 19.1 points · named by 15/15

  7. 7

    Max PlanckMax Planck 1858–19471858–1947

    Z uvedbo kvanta energije pri razlagi sevanja črnega telesa je utemeljil kvantno teorijo; leta 1918 je prejel Nobelovo nagrado za fiziko.

    Founded quantum theory by introducing the energy quantum to explain black-body radiation; awarded the 1918 Nobel Prize in Physics.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Planck, Berlin 1900: pred Nemškim fizikalnim društvom predstavi zakon sevanja s kvanti energijePlanck, Berlin 1900: presents his radiation law with energy quanta to the German Physical Society

    Ø 17,4 točke · omenilo 14/15avg 17.4 points · named by 14/15

  8. 8

    Erwin SchrödingerErwin Schrödinger 1887–19611887–1961

    Postavil je valovno enačbo kvantne mehanike, ki opisuje razvoj kvantnih sistemov; leta 1933 je prejel Nobelovo nagrado za fiziko.

    Formulated the wave equation of quantum mechanics describing how quantum systems evolve; Nobel Prize in Physics 1933.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Schrödinger, Zürich 1926: objavi valovno enačbo kvantne mehanikeSchrödinger, Zurich 1926: publishes the wave equation of quantum mechanics

    Ø 17,3 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 17.3 points · named by 15/15

  9. 9

    Michael FaradayMichael Faraday 1791–18671791–1867

    Odkril je elektromagnetno indukcijo, na kateri temeljijo generatorji in transformatorji, ter zakone elektrolize.

    Discovered electromagnetic induction, the basis of generators and transformers, and the laws of electrolysis.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Faraday, London 1831: tok iz magnetizmaFaraday, London 1831: electric current from magnetism

    Ø 16,5 točke · omenilo 14/15avg 16.5 points · named by 14/15

  10. 10

    Richard FeynmanRichard Feynman 1918–19881918–1988

    Soustvaril je kvantno elektrodinamiko in uvedel Feynmanove diagrame; leta 1965 je s Schwingerjem in Tomonago prejel Nobelovo nagrado za fiziko.

    Co-developed quantum electrodynamics and introduced Feynman diagrams; shared the 1965 Nobel Prize in Physics with Schwinger and Tomonaga.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Feynman, Washington 1986: s kozarcem ledene vode pokaže, zakaj je na raketoplanu Challenger odpovedalo tesniloFeynman, Washington 1986: ice-water demonstration shows why a seal failed on the Space Shuttle Challenger

    Ø 16,4 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 16.4 points · named by 15/15

  11. 11

    Paul DiracPaul Dirac 1902–19841902–1984

    Postavil je relativistično kvantno enačbo za elektron, s katero je napovedal obstoj antimaterije, in soustvaril kvantno mehaniko.

    Formulated the relativistic quantum equation of the electron, which predicted antimatter, and helped build quantum mechanics.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Dirac, Cambridge 1928: objavi relativistično kvantno enačbo za elektronDirac, Cambridge 1928: publishes the relativistic quantum equation of the electron

    Ø 16,1 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 16.1 points · named by 15/15

  12. 12

    Marie CurieMarie Curie 1867–19341867–1934

    Raziskovala je radioaktivnost, odkrila polonij in radij ter prva prejela Nobelovo nagrado na dveh znanstvenih področjih.

    Pioneered research on radioactivity, discovered polonium and radium and first won Nobel Prizes in two sciences.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Curie, Pariz 1898: s Pierrom odkrije polonij in radijCurie, Paris 1898: with Pierre, discovers polonium and radium

    Ø 14,7 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 14.7 points · named by 15/15

  13. 13

    Ludwig BoltzmannLudwig Boltzmann 1844–19061844–1906

    Soutemeljil je statistično mehaniko in entropijo pojasnil s številom mikroskopskih stanj, ki ustrezajo makroskopskemu stanju sistema.

    Co-founded statistical mechanics and explained entropy in terms of the number of microscopic states corresponding to a system's macroscopic state.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Boltzmann, 1877: entropijo poveže z verjetnostjo oziroma številom mikrostanjBoltzmann, 1877: links entropy to probability and the number of microstates

    Ø 11,9 točke · omenilo 14/15avg 11.9 points · named by 14/15

  14. 14

    Enrico FermiEnrico Fermi 1901–19541901–1954

    Razvil je teorijo razpada beta, raziskoval jedrske reakcije s počasnimi nevtroni in vodil gradnjo prvega jedrskega reaktorja.

    Developed the theory of beta decay, studied nuclear reactions induced by slow neutrons and led the construction of the first nuclear reactor.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Fermi, Chicago 1942: reaktor Chicago Pile-1 doseže prvo nadzorovano jedrsko verižno reakcijoFermi, Chicago 1942: Chicago Pile-1 achieves the first controlled nuclear chain reaction

    Ø 11,1 točke · omenilo 14/15avg 11.1 points · named by 14/15

  15. 15

    Ernest RutherfordErnest Rutherford 1871–19371871–1937

    Odkril je atomsko jedro, razlikoval sevanji alfa in beta ter prvi umetno sprožil jedrsko pretvorbo; leta 1908 je prejel Nobelovo nagrado za kemijo.

    Discovered the atomic nucleus, distinguished alpha and beta radiation and achieved the first artificial nuclear transmutation; Nobel Prize in Chemistry 1908.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Rutherford, Manchester 1911: na podlagi sipanja delcev alfa predlaga model atoma z jedromRutherford, Manchester 1911: proposes the nuclear model of the atom based on alpha-particle scattering

    Ø 11,0 točke · omenilo 13/15avg 11.0 points · named by 13/15

  16. 16

    Christiaan HuygensChristiaan Huygens 1629–16951629–1695

    Razvil je valovno teorijo svetlobe, izumil nihalno uro in odkril Saturnovo luno Titan.

    Developed the wave theory of light, invented the pendulum clock and discovered Saturn's moon Titan.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Huygens, Haag 1656: izdela prvo nihalno uroHuygens, The Hague 1656: builds the first pendulum clock

    Ø 9,0 točke · omenilo 13/15avg 9.0 points · named by 13/15

  17. 17

    Wolfgang PauliWolfgang Pauli 1900–19581900–1958

    Formuliral je izključitveno načelo, leta 1930 napovedal obstoj nevtrina in leta 1945 prejel Nobelovo nagrado za fiziko.

    Formulated the exclusion principle, predicted the neutrino in 1930 and received the 1945 Nobel Prize in Physics.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Pauli, Hamburg 1925: formulira izključitveno načelo za elektrone v atomuPauli, Hamburg 1925: formulates the exclusion principle for electrons in atoms

    Ø 6,1 točke · omenilo 12/15avg 6.1 points · named by 12/15

  18. 18

    Hendrik Antoon LorentzHendrik Lorentz 1853–19281853–1928

    Razvil je elektronsko teorijo snovi in transformacije prostora in časa, ki so postale temelj Einsteinove posebne teorije relativnosti.

    Developed the electron theory of matter and the space-time transformations that became a foundation of Einstein's special relativity.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Lorentz, 1902: s Pieterjem Zeemanom prejme Nobelovo nagrado za fizikoLorentz, 1902: shares the Nobel Prize in Physics with Pieter Zeeman

    Ø 4,1 točke · omenilo 9/15avg 4.1 points · named by 9/15

  19. 19

    Nicolas Léonard Sadi CarnotNicolas Léonard Sadi Carnot 1796–18321796–1832

    Z opisom Carnotovega krožnega procesa je določil teoretično mejo izkoristka toplotnih strojev in postavil temelje termodinamike.

    Described the Carnot cycle, establishing the theoretical limit on the efficiency of heat engines and laying the foundations of thermodynamics.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Carnot, Pariz 1824: izide delo Razmišljanja o gonilni sili ognjaCarnot, Paris 1824: Reflections on the Motive Power of Fire published

    Ø 3,9 točke · omenilo 6/15avg 3.9 points · named by 6/15

  20. 20

    Joseph John ThomsonJ. J. Thomson 1856–19401856–1940

    Odkril je elektron in s tem pokazal, da ima atom notranjo zgradbo; leta 1906 je prejel Nobelovo nagrado za fiziko.

    Discovered the electron, showing that the atom has an internal structure; awarded the 1906 Nobel Prize in Physics.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Thomson, Cambridge 1897: s poskusi s katodnimi žarki odkrije elektronThomson, Cambridge 1897: discovers the electron in cathode-ray experiments

    Ø 3,1 točke · omenilo 7/15avg 3.1 points · named by 7/15

  21. 21

    William Thomson KelvinLord Kelvin 1824–19071824–1907

    Predlagal je absolutno temperaturno lestvico, prispeval k utemeljitvi termodinamike in znanstveno svetoval pri polaganju čezatlantskega telegrafskega kabla.

    Proposed the absolute temperature scale, helped establish thermodynamics and advised on the laying of the transatlantic telegraph cable.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Kelvin, 1848: kot William Thomson predlaga absolutno temperaturno lestvicoKelvin, 1848: as William Thomson he proposes an absolute temperature scale

    Ø 3,1 točke · omenilo 5/15avg 3.1 points · named by 5/15

  22. 22

    Murray Gell-MannMurray Gell-Mann 1929–20191929–2019

    Razvrstil je hadrone po simetriji in predlagal model kvarkov; leta 1969 je prejel Nobelovo nagrado za fiziko.

    Classified hadrons by symmetry (the Eightfold Way) and proposed the quark model; Nobel Prize in Physics 1969.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Gell-Mann, Pasadena 1964: predlaga obstoj kvarkovGell-Mann, Pasadena 1964: proposes the existence of quarks

    Ø 2,9 točke · omenilo 8/15avg 2.9 points · named by 8/15

  23. 23

    J. Robert OppenheimerJ. Robert Oppenheimer 1904–19671904–1967

    Kot znanstveni direktor laboratorija Los Alamos je v projektu Manhattan vodil razvoj prve jedrske bombe.

    As scientific director of the Los Alamos laboratory, led the Manhattan Project's development of the first atomic bomb.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Oppenheimer, Nova Mehika 1945: preizkus Trinity, prva eksplozija jedrske bombeOppenheimer, New Mexico 1945: Trinity test, the first nuclear explosion

    Ø 2,7 točke · omenilo 6/15avg 2.7 points · named by 6/15

  24. 24

    James Prescott JouleJames Prescott Joule 1818–18891818–1889

    S poskusi je določil mehanski ekvivalent toplote in podprl zakon o ohranitvi energije; po njem se imenuje enota za energijo joule.

    Measured the mechanical equivalent of heat, supporting the law of conservation of energy; the unit of energy, the joule, is named after him.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Joule, Cambridge 1845: predstavi poskus z lopatastim kolesom v vodi za merjenje mehanskega ekvivalenta toploteJoule, Cambridge 1845: presents his paddle-wheel experiment measuring the mechanical equivalent of heat

    Ø 2,6 točke · omenilo 4/15avg 2.6 points · named by 4/15

  25. 25

    André-Marie AmpèreAndré-Marie Ampère 1775–18361775–1836

    Utemeljil je elektrodinamiko in matematično opisal magnetno silo med vodniki s tokom; po njem se imenuje enota amper.

    Founded electrodynamics and mathematically described the magnetic force between current-carrying conductors; the unit ampere is named after him.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Ampère, Pariz 1820: pokaže, da se vzporedna vodnika s tokom privlačita ali odbijataAmpère, Paris 1820: shows that parallel current-carrying wires attract or repel each other

    Ø 2,3 točke · omenilo 5/15avg 2.3 points · named by 5/15

1b · ZnanostScience

AstronomijaAstronomy

Astronomija, astrofizika in kozmologija, opazovanje neba od antike do danes.

Astronomy, astrophysics and cosmology, watching the sky from antiquity to today.

Modeli so predlagali 77 različnih oseb; tu je prvih 25.The models proposed 77 different people; the top 25 are shown here.

  1. 1

    Nikolaj KopernikNicolaus Copernicus 1473–15431473–1543

    Postavil je heliocentrični model, v katerem Zemlja in drugi planeti krožijo okoli Sonca.

    Proposed the heliocentric model in which Earth and the other planets orbit the Sun.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Kopernik, Nürnberg 1543: izide delo O kroženju nebesnih sferCopernicus, Nuremberg 1543: De revolutionibus published

    Ø 24,4 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 24.4 points · named by 15/15

  2. 2

    Johannes KeplerJohannes Kepler 1571–16301571–1630

    Odkril je tri zakone gibanja planetov, med njimi spoznanje, da se planeti gibljejo okoli Sonca po elipsah.

    Discovered the three laws of planetary motion, including that the planets move around the Sun in ellipses.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Kepler, 1609: izide Astronomia nova s prvima dvema zakonoma gibanja planetovKepler, 1609: Astronomia nova with the first two laws of planetary motion published

    Ø 22,1 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 22.1 points · named by 15/15

  3. 3

    Tycho BraheTycho Brahe 1546–16011546–1601

    Brez teleskopa je opravil natančne in sistematične meritve položajev zvezd in planetov, iz katerih je Kepler izpeljal zakone gibanja planetov.

    Made precise, systematic naked-eye measurements of star and planet positions, which Kepler used to derive his laws of planetary motion.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Brahe, 1572: opazuje novo zvezdo v ozvezdju Kasiopeje in pokaže, da leži onkraj LuneBrahe, 1572: observes a new star in Cassiopeia and shows that it lies beyond the Moon

    Ø 21,4 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 21.4 points · named by 15/15

  4. 4

    Edwin HubbleEdwin Hubble 1889–19531889–1953

    Pokazal je, da so spiralne meglice galaksije zunaj Rimske ceste, in ugotovil, da se galaksije oddaljujejo s hitrostjo, sorazmerno njihovi razdalji.

    Showed that spiral nebulae are galaxies beyond the Milky Way and established that galaxies recede at speeds proportional to their distance.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Hubble, Mount Wilson 1929: objavi zvezo med razdaljo galaksij in hitrostjo njihovega oddaljevanjaHubble, Mount Wilson 1929: publishes the relation between galaxy distance and recession velocity

    Ø 20,3 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 20.3 points · named by 15/15

  5. 5

    William HerschelWilliam Herschel 1738–18221738–1822

    Odkril je planet Uran, katalogiziral na tisoče meglic in zvezdnih kopic ter odkril infrardeče sevanje.

    Discovered the planet Uranus, catalogued thousands of nebulae and star clusters and discovered infrared radiation.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Herschel, Bath 1781: s svojim teleskopom odkrije planet UranHerschel, Bath 1781: discovers the planet Uranus with his own telescope

    Ø 18,1 točke · omenilo 15/15avg 18.1 points · named by 15/15

  6. 6

    Klavdij PtolemajPtolemy ok. 100–ok. 170c. 100–c. 170 AD

    V Almagestu je sistematično izdelal geocentrični model vesolja, ki je v astronomiji prevladoval več kot tisoč let; napisal je tudi Geografijo.

    Systematized the geocentric model of the universe in the Almagest, which dominated astronomy for over a thousand years; also wrote the Geography.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Ptolemaj, Aleksandrija ok. 150: nastane Almagest z geocentričnim modelom vesoljaPtolemy, Alexandria c. 150: the Almagest, with its geocentric model, is written

    Ø 17,5 točke · omenilo 13/15avg 17.5 points · named by 13/15

  7. 7

    HiparhHipparchus ok. 190–120 pr. n. št.c. 190–120 BC

    Sestavil je enega prvih zvezdnih katalogov, odkril precesijo enakonočij in razvil zgodnjo trigonometrijo.

    Compiled one of the first star catalogues, discovered the precession of the equinoxes and developed early trigonometry.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Hiparh, Rodos ok. 130 pr. n. št.: odkrije precesijo enakonočijHipparchus, Rhodes c. 130 BC: discovers the precession of the equinoxes

    Ø 15,7 točke · omenilo 13/15avg 15.7 points · named by 13/15

  8. 8

    Carl SaganCarl Sagan 1934–19961934–1996

    Planetarni astronom, ki je sodeloval pri vesoljskih misijah NASA in z nanizanko Cosmos približal znanost širši javnosti.

    Planetary scientist who worked on NASA space missions and brought science to a wide public through the TV series Cosmos.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Sagan, 1990: na njegovo pobudo sonda Voyager 1 posname Zemljo kot bledo modro pikoSagan, 1990: at his urging Voyager 1 photographs Earth as the Pale Blue Dot

    Ø 12,0 točke · omenilo 12/15avg 12.0 points · named by 12/15

  9. 9

    Georges LemaîtreGeorges Lemaître 1894–19661894–1966

    Iz splošne teorije relativnosti je izpeljal širjenje vesolja in predlagal zamisel o »prvobitnem atomu«, zametek teorije velikega poka.

    Derived the expansion of the universe from general relativity and proposed the 'primeval atom', the precursor of the Big Bang theory.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Lemaître, 1927: objavi članek o širjenju vesolja, izpeljanem iz splošne relativnostiLemaître, 1927: publishes his paper on an expanding universe derived from general relativity

    Ø 11,7 točke · omenilo 12/15avg 11.7 points · named by 12/15

  10. 10

    Subrahmanjan ČandrasekarSubrahmanyan Chandrasekhar 1910–19951910–1995

    Izračunal je največjo možno maso bele pritlikavke, imenovano Čandrasekarjeva meja, in raziskoval zgradbo zvezd; leta 1983 je prejel Nobelovo nagrado za fiziko.

    Calculated the maximum possible mass of a white dwarf, known as the Chandrasekhar limit, and studied stellar structure; Nobel Prize in Physics 1983.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Čandrasekar, 1930: na plovbi iz Indije v Anglijo izračuna mejno maso belih pritlikavkChandrasekhar, 1930: on the voyage from India to England calculates the mass limit of white dwarfs

    Ø 11,6 točke · omenilo 13/15avg 11.6 points · named by 13/15

  11. 11

    Annie Jump CannonAnnie Jump Cannon 1863–19411863–1941

    Razvila je harvardsko shemo spektralne klasifikacije zvezd (O, B, A, F, G, K, M) in razvrstila na stotisoče zvezd.

    Developed the Harvard spectral classification of stars (O, B, A, F, G, K, M) and classified hundreds of thousands of stars.

    Ključni trenutekKey moment Cannon, 1922: Mednarodna astronomska zveza sprejme njeno shemo spektralne klasifikacijeCannon, 1922: the International Astronomical Union adopts her spectral classification scheme

07 · PodročjeField

Pravo in pravna državaLaw and the rule of law

Pravni misleci, pisci zakonikov in ustav, sodniki in pravniki, utemeljitelji človekovih pravic v pravu; vladarji, katerih glavni prispevek je vladanje, so med državniki.

Legal thinkers, authors of law codes and constitutions, judges and jurists, founders of human rights in law; rulers whose main contribution is governing are among statespeople.

Geographical discoveries, expeditions, exploration of the oceans, the polar regions and space, including astronauts; scientific explanations belong to science.

21 · Posebno področjeSpecial field

ZloglasniThe Infamous

Tirani, množični morilci in povzročitelji največjega trpljenja.

Tyrants, mass murderers and those who caused the greatest suffering.

To ni lestvica časti, temveč opomin na največje zlo, ki so ga ljudje prizadejali ljudem. Vrstni red sledi obsegu povzročene škode in trpljenja po mnenju modelov.

This is not a roll of honour but a warning about the greatest evil people have done to people. The order follows the extent of the harm and suffering caused, as judged by the models.

DeepSeek je ta seznam dal šele po navodilu, naj izpusti kitajske osebe, zato se pri kitajskih osebah na tem seznamu ne šteje (N = 14).

DeepSeek gave this list only after being told to leave out Chinese people, so it is not counted for the Chinese people on this list (N = 14).

Modeli so predlagali 78 različnih oseb; tu je prvih 25.The models proposed 78 different people; the top 25 are shown here.

    Nazi dictator of Germany (1933–1945) who started the Second World War in Europe and directed the Holocaust, in which about six million Jews were murdered.

MetodaMethod

Kako je seznam nastalHow the list was made

Odgovori so bili zbrani 29. in 30. 10. 2026. Vsak od 15 jezikovnih modelov je odgovarjal v svojem spletnem klepetu z najnižjo nastavitvijo, v enem pogovoru s šestimi sporočili. Prvo je vsebovalo pravila in vsa področja, vsako sporočilo pa je zahtevalo en del seznamov. Pravila: 25 oseb na seznam, vsaka oseba samo enkrat in na področju svojega glavnega prispevka, samo posamezniki. Vrstni red so modeli določali po trajnem vplivu, izvirnosti in dosežkih, pri Zloglasnih pa po obsegu povzročene škode in trpljenja; odgovarjali so iz lastnega znanja, brez spletnega iskanja.

The answers were collected on 29 and 30 Oct 2026. Each of the 15 language models answered in its own web chat on its lowest setting, in one conversation of six messages. The first set out the rules and all the fields, and each message asked for one part of the lists. The rules: 25 people per list, each person only once and in the field of their main contribution, individuals only. The models ranked people by lasting influence, originality and achievement, and the Infamous by the extent of harm and suffering caused; they answered from their own knowledge, without web search.

DolaChat
  1. Po vsakem delu je samodejna kontrola preverila število imen, podvojitve in skupine. Ob napaki je model dobil popravek, naj podvojeno osebo umakne in doda novo; na vsak del sta bila mogoča največ dva popravka.
  2. Točke: mesto r prinese 26 − r točk (1. mesto 25, … 25. mesto 1), neomenjena oseba dobi 0. Upošteva se povprečje čez N modelov: to so modeli, ki so oddali ta del, in vsak drug model, ki je osebo navedel na drugem področju.
  3. Vsak model da osebi največ en rezultat; če jo je navedel dvakrat, šteje prva navedba. Oseba gre na seznam, kjer ima največ točk.
  4. Razvrstitev: točke, nato število modelov, ki so osebo omenili, nato najboljše mesto.
  5. Imena: 2490 različnih zapisov je poenotenih v identitete, tako da različni prepisi in oblike istega imena štejejo kot ena oseba.
  1. After each part an automatic check counted the names and looked for duplicates and groups. If something was wrong, the model got a correction asking it to drop the duplicate and add someone new; each part allowed at most two corrections.
  2. Points: place r earns 26 − r points (1st place 25, … 25th place 1), and a person not named gets 0. The score is the average over N models: those that submitted that part plus any other model that named the person in another field.
  3. Each model gives a person at most one result; if it named someone twice, the first mention counts. A person goes to the list where they score the most points.
  4. Order: points, then the number of models that named the person, then the best place.
  5. Names: 2,490 different spellings were merged into identities, so that transliterations and variants of the same name count as one person.

Vrstica pod osebo: Ø = povprečne točke (največ 25); omenilo x/N = koliko od N modelov je osebo navedlo na katerem koli seznamu.

The line under each person: avg = average points (at most 25); named by x/N = how many of the N models listed the person anywhere.

OdstopanjaDeviations

  • DeepSeek je dela s področji 7–11 in 21 sprva zavrnil (»beyond my current scope«). Po posredovanju BD je del 3 oddal kot pozitivni seznam; na njegovem seznamu državnikov ni nobene kitajske osebe. Zloglasne je dal šele po navodilu, naj izpusti kitajske osebe, zato se pri kitajskih osebah na tem seznamu ne šteje (N = 14).
  • Cohere se je v dolgem pogovoru zaciklal. Dele 3–6 je dobil v novih sejah, po eno za vsak del; del 2 (področja 2–6) je izločen, zato je tam N = 14 (oziroma 15 pri osebah, ki jih je Cohere navedel na drugem področju).
  • DeepSeek at first refused the parts with fields 7–11 and 21 (“beyond my current scope”). After BD stepped in, it submitted part 3 as a positive list; its statespeople list contains no Chinese person. It gave the Infamous list only after being told to leave out Chinese people, so it is not counted for the Chinese people on that list (N = 14).
  • Cohere got stuck in a loop in the long conversation. It received parts 3–6 in fresh sessions, one per part; part 2 (fields 2–6) is excluded, so N = 14 there (15 for people Cohere named in another field).

Kdo je odločil kajWho decided what

  • BD: pobuda za seznam; razdelitev na 20 področij s petimi podpodročji znanosti in posebnim področjem Zloglasni; odločitev, da osnutek gre na splet takoj in se sproti dopolnjuje; zamisel, da seznam napaja igro.
  • Modeli: vse uvrstitve. Točke so izračunane samodejno iz odgovorov vseh 15 modelov.
  • Claude (Opus 5.5): opisi, letnice in ključni trenutki na tej strani; za osebe, ki so bile že v R1, iz 29. 10., za druge iz 30. 10. 2026. Še niso pregledani.
  • BD: the idea for the list; the split into 20 fields with five science sub-fields and the special Infamous field; the decision to put the draft online right away and extend it as we go; the idea that the list feeds the game.
  • Models: all rankings. The points are computed automatically from the answers of all 15 models.
  • Claude (Opus 5.5): the descriptions, years and key moments on this page; for people already in R1 from 29 Oct, for the others from 30 Oct 2026. Not yet reviewed.

  • in preparation Review: the descriptions, years and key moments await review.
Za igroFor the game

Po čigavi poti izumljamo z AIWhose path we invent along with AI

Isti seznam je strojno berljiv v datoteki people.json. Igra iz njega izbira osebo, po katere poti z AI znova izumljamo. V polju people so vsa področja razen Zloglasnih, urejena po mestu: najprej vsi s 1. mesta, nato vsi z 2. mesta in tako naprej, zato je med prvimi 300 vrh vseh področij. Vsaka oseba ima stalen id, ki se med verzijami seznama ne spreminja, da povezave v igri ostanejo veljavne; osebe iz R1 so svojega obdržale.

The same list is machine-readable in people.json. The game picks a person from it whose path we re-invent with AI. The people array holds every field except the Infamous, ordered by place: first everyone in 1st place, then everyone in 2nd place and so on, so the first 300 cover the top of every field. Each person has a permanent id that does not change between list versions, so links in the game stay valid; people from R1 kept theirs.
